# Data completeness audit — what the releases hold, what is on disk, what is used, and why the difference

Greg, 2026-09-23: *"are there any more datasets I am truncating because of stupid fucking reasons?"* — then:
*"literally every instance of these stupid mistakes should be fixed or justified why they aren't included
before rerunning, except FluoroCap."* Eight dataset groups were audited against their release pages and the
grid; five were independently re-counted by a second agent. **The run hit the model limit: the twelve
verification and repair agents named at the end never ran, and the synthesis is this file, written from the
structured results.** No repair job was launched by the audit itself.

Reason classes: **bug** (a defect in our code or a truncated file), **budget** (a download or compute cut with
no criterion), **unfinished** (a job or push that never completed), **unknown** (nobody can say), **criterion**
(a scientific reason written down somewhere — quoted).

- repair: Before any retrain: exclude names ending _v2/_ap45 (or a 'derived: true' key in data.yaml) in ready(), or move the derived banks out of realxray/; delete them once the full export supersedes them; add a test that no two ready datasets share a case id (tools/leak_check.py already keys cobra by case).

- **VerSe 19 + VerSe 20 (Sekuboyina 2021 MedIA; Loeffler 2021 Sc** — Release -> disk (label count, not a truncation): the paper's 85 six-lumbar scans vs 50 scans carrying label 25 in the masks (35 table rows without an L6 label: 34 Castellvi III/IV rows ). Criterion: VerSe's own annotation protocol, written in the descriptor: 'the sacrum including lumbar transitional vertebrae, i.e. vertebrae partially fused with the sacrum, have not been segmented in this dataset' — so 46 of the 85 rows (48 series with multi-series subjects) carry a segmented L6 and the fused ones never will; the 6 T13 agree exactly between paper and masks. 
- **VerSe 19 + VerSe 20 (Sekuboyina 2021 MedIA; Loeffler 2021 Sc** — Disk -> used: VerSeFusion manifest flags (has_L6 44, has_T13 18) disagree with the raw masks (50 / 6) because stage 6 VERIDAH renumbered 14 scans (six native L6 scans became 'T13 + L5', six more L5-lowest scans became T13); the pipeline reads the raw masks (correct: they match the published 6 T13),  (0 scans; 14 scans whose level names differ between the two t). Criterion: A deliberate, documented pipeline stage ('Runs VERIDAH as a referee over every scan, correcting 14 enumeration-anomaly label assignments', README) whose output lives in data/corrected and the manifest while unified keeps VerSe's labels; the trap is two label trees under one name, not a wrong label map as the memory note says. 
- **VerSe 19 + VerSe 20 (Sekuboyina 2021 MedIA; Loeffler 2021 Sc** — Disk -> used: ~/lstv_flag/renders_verse covers 288 of 374 series (86 series). Criterion: Written in tools/lstv_flag_render_verse.py: 'the lumbar and junction framings need a sacrum or an L5, the wide framing needs T12. A case that supports none is skipped and named in the log' — a field-of-view requirement for a lumbosacral flag. 

## Unknown — nobody can say why

- **COBRA2026 (Thummerer et al. 2026, arXiv:2607.20037, Zenodo 1** — 24 frames per case out of a 190-734-frame sweep: N_FRAMES = 24 hard-coded, frames = round(i * nproj / 24) i.e. one frame per 15 deg of a 360-deg orbit (per ~8 deg on the 7 half-orbit A cases); laterals included (74,142 of the 77,622 projections of the 150 attempted cases ): No reason is written anywhere: not in the spec, the datasheet, the resume or the commit (147428d 'COBRA exporter (agent-built)'); the angular selection is deterministic and even (view bins 542-731 frames each), so the SUBSET is angle-stratified, but 24 is a magic number. It was not a hard compute limit: median 137 s per case for 24 frames (5.7 s/frame incl. the pose-QC raycast), so a full 472-fram
- **FluoroCap — literature/web harvest of published spine fluoro** — Bank built from ONE decode file (decode_v2_v4: 6,480 panels inferred) instead of the union of decodes (20,700 inferred, 11,475 labelled). The all-panels + video decode (hires_allspine_v2_r2, 19,625 inferred / 10,500 labelled) never fed a bank. (3,695 panels with >=3 labels are labelled in the union but a): tools/fluorocap_to_ann.py defaults to --decode decode_v2_v4.jsonl and its docstring gives no reason for choosing v4 over the union that LEVEL_COUNTS.md and the corpus gate both use.
- **GROUP: data on the machine used by nothing, and fetches that** — Mendeley 5jdfdgp762: page says 716 images (494/206/16), the served Dataset.zip holds 714 (494/204/16) (2 validation images (release-side)): Our copy is byte-complete (43,892,677 bytes = the API's size; testzip clean); the discrepancy is inside the release between its description and its one file.
- **GROUP: data on the machine used by nothing, and fetches that** — cm_spine: the download never landed and nothing records what the file is (everything): download_sources.py get_cm_spine() gdown of Drive file 1r9wX1802gkU4-EQHMMlyAlEiWXGWLhhJ left an empty directory on 2026-09-01; the only description is 'Google Drive file, intraoperative'.
- **Plain-film corpora: CSXA (cervical), BUU-LSPINE (incl. the l** — BUU-LSPINE AP films: 3,600 of the 7,200 released images are outside the level bank (only the 3,603 laterals train it) (3,600 AP films (50 % of the release) — yet the manuscript's ): No written criterion anywhere for excluding AP from the level bank: launch_dual.sh only says the AP set is a 'relative experiment', MANIFEST line 165 says 'only the 3,603 laterals' with no reason, and the fold banks do take AP frames from C-ArmLSpine, so view is not a stated exclusion rule.
- **Plain-film corpora: CSXA (cervical), BUU-LSPINE (incl. the l** — BUU-LSPINE(2000) second label vintage (side-bending/rotation, flexion/extension diagnoses; 1,337 of 4,000 csv differ from buu_full) held on disk as buu_2000 and unused; local BUU-LSPINE_400 sample folder unused (0 images (both are subsets of buu_full by pixels); 1,337 rel): buu_2000 is mislabelled 'AASCE test split … report spreadsheet only' in tools/xray_dataset_usage.py RAW list (and so in docs/XRAY_DATASET_USAGE.md), so nobody knew what it was; the local 400 folder (v0.1 sample, 889 MB, untracked) is an orphan download referenced by no code.
- **Real 3-D-truth corpora group: C-ArmLSpine, DeepFluoro (regis** — C-ArmLSpine case39 second lateral exposure dropped (1 frame (case39: two 90 deg laterals at 89 kV/25 mA; the lat): carmlspine_labels._frames() keeps the first frame per view by code, and no document states that a case contributes exactly one AP and one lateral or why a repeat exposure is excluded (the DATASHEET only says '148 AP + 148 lateral').
- **Real 3-D-truth corpora group: C-ArmLSpine, DeepFluoro (regis** — DeepFluoro vendor fluoroscopy frames and vendor CTs unused (2,293 of 2,659 vendor fluoroscopy frames (2,659 released min): The realxray export (2026-09-21 17:59) was built on the H5 before the vendor deposit landed (2026-09-22 01:23-01:35); the obvious criterion — only the 366 registered frames carry a ground-truth pose, so only they yield projected truth — is written nowhere, and DATASHEET/manifest describe the deposit as 'vendor DICOM headers of the same frames', which is false (it is ~7x the frames plus 19 CTs).
- **Real 3-D-truth corpora group: C-ArmLSpine, DeepFluoro (regis** — DeepFluoro frames used at x4 downsample; CT from the H5, not the vendor originals (resolution, not count: 1536^2 at 0.194 mm -> 384^2 at 0.776 ): tools/realxray_deepfluoro_export.py documents the x4 downsample and the h5 CT as choices but gives no reason; C-ArmLSpine real frames enter at 1024^2 and the detector trains at imgsz 1024, so DeepFluoro real frames are the only ones entering at 384^2 — a mismatch no document states.
- **Real 3-D-truth corpora group: C-ArmLSpine, DeepFluoro (regis** — DeepFluoro fiducial and sawbones objects in the vendor deposit unused (601 files (09_ftrac_fiducial: CT + 72 fluoro files) and 5,85): PATIENT_IDENTITY_SPEC says only that they are objects and get no person key; nothing states they are excluded from imaging use or why (a tracking fiducial has no spine; the sawbones pelvis may carry L5/sacrum).
- **TotalSegmentator v2.0.1 (Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.10047292) and** — v3 CTs -> ts_drr_v3: the 29 'no silhouette' and the 39 gap/disordered skips (68 (29 no silhouette: 13 ct head, 10 blank, 3 ct operation .): 'no silhouette' means a vertebra mask exists but project_labels returned no level, a mechanism nobody has looked at; the gap/disordered rule is written for the v2 mix-ups, yet v3 says those are fixed, so whether these 39 are residual label errors or a centroid-order artefact on head/neck scans has not been checked by rendering (the project's own rule: render the mask).
- **TotalSegmentator v2.0.1 (Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.10047292) and** — devcorpus renders per geometry vs subjects x per_subject (14 (elekta_xvi 1,122 of 1,136), 25 (cios_fusion 1,537 of 1,5): RENDER_INFO.json records renders, subjects and per_subject but no failure list, so which subject-pose draws were lost and why is not recorded.
- **TotalSegmentator v2.0.1 (Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.10047292) and** — v2 CTs 1,228 -> physics_drr TS renders (1,831 anns over 1,121 subjects), the TS share of the level bank (107 subjects: 37 are the render-skipped no-spine scans, 70 s): The min-levels rule is written in render_physics_drr.py ('drop a render with fewer unclipped vertebrae than this'), but no log from 2026-09-14 survives, so which of the 70 fell to that rule and which to an exception is not known.
- **TotalSegmentator v2.0.1 (Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.10047292) and** — v3 CTs 1,939 -> anchor_drr_c2 TS images (3,779 over 1,890 subjects) (49 subjects with no image and one subject with one view; 0 '): make_anchor_drr.py drops a case silently (bare continue) when no anchor-class structure projects, so the 49 are presumably scans with no vertebra, sacrum, femur, rib or skull mask, but nothing on disk says so.
- **VerSe 19 + VerSe 20 (Sekuboyina 2021 MedIA; Loeffler 2021 Sc** — Release -> disk: centroid JSONs (374) and snapshot PNGs are downloaded to data/raw but not carried into data/unified (materialize_kinds ct/msk/snp, required ct/msk; 150 scans have no snp at all) (0 scans; 2 of the 4 shipped file types not in the working tr): Nothing downstream reads centroids (levels are derived from the masks) and no doc states why unified omits them; the raw copies remain on disk.

Documents that disagree with the disk:
- B000's exclusion reason. docs/DATASHEET.md 197, NUMBERS.md 106, CLAIM_CHECKLIST.md 99, claim_checklist.json 106/168/444, atlas/dataset.json Noise (8) all say '145 kept (B000 dropped: 41 frames the CT never covers)'. The disk (cobra_elekta/cases/B000.json, summary.json cases.excluded) says B000 was excluded by the AXIS-MAP gate (map0 edge NCC -0.785, best other -0.691) together with A081, A239, A26
- Job id of the full fetch. DATASHEET.md 47/186, RSNAI_TABLE1 and SchwingNet/results/JOBS_cobra.txt name array 40416296 (one task per centre, five files); the running fetch is 40416315 (18 tasks, every file) chained to verify 40416316 -- squeue shows 40416315_0..17 RUNNING and 40416316 PENDING; RESUME_2026-09-22_PM.md 1113 records the supersession. JOBS_cobra.txt and the datasheet are stale.
- On-disk size. DATASHEET.md 47, RSNAI_TABLE1 rows 7 AND 8 (the Varian row repeats the Elekta cell) and RSNAI_DATA_MANIFEST.json say '988 files, 42.2 GB' -- the manifest of 2026-09-22 06:49, mid-fetch. At this census: 351 case dirs, 157 GB and rising; the manifest must be rebuilt after COBRA_COMPLETE.md.
- Elekta/Varian totals. docs/HANDOFF_2026-09-21_PM.md 607 ('634 Elekta XVI + 283 Varian'), quoted in RESUME 1109, disagrees with the paper's table: Elekta 150+134+150+150 = 584, Varian 116+167 = 283 over all 867; public = 467 Elekta + 225 Varian. 634 is wrong.
- Varian device card. SchwingNet/CLAIM_CHECKLIST.md 103 and claim_checklist.json 447 claim a varian_halcyon card fitted on 525 paired frames; ls docs/devices/ shows no varian_halcyon.json (RESUME A11 noticed, not fixed). The Varian cases contribute nothing to any file that exists.
- Selection wording. summary.json 'selection: per centre, sorted Elekta case ids, ids[floor(i*n/N)] ... N = 40/40/40/30' presents the 150 as a systematic sample; DATASHEET 181 says the cut was download volume. Both can be true (a budget applied evenly), but D = 30 rather than 40 has no explanation in either place.
- Spec vs disk. docs/REAL_XRAY_DATASETS_SPEC.md lists `cobra_varian` among the five datasets; no such bank exists under /rs/rs_grp_oschome/go2432/data/realxray/.
- The 24 frames per case appear in no document at all: only summary.json 'frames_per_case: 24' and the tool docstring ('24 frames evenly over the orbit'); tab_corpus.md and the datasheet quote 3,480 frames without saying they are 24 of a median 472 per sweep (4.5 %).

Documents that disagree with the disk:
- docs/DATASHEET.md section 2.7, docs/MODEL_CARD.md section 3.1 and docs/RSNAI_DATA_MANIFEST.json ts_subjects.source call the device-corpus source 'TotalSegmentator v2'; it is v3.0.0: tools/ts_clean_subjects.py ROOT is the v300 folder, the manifest path is the v300 folder, 228,803 files = 1,939 x 118 + meta.csv, and 140 of the 142 ids are v3 renders while only 44 exist in the v2 bank. Disk is right;
- docs/CT_INVENTORY.md 'TotalSegmentator v3 | 0 nii | 1 dirs' is wrong: tools/ct_inventory.py counts one level below ~/data/TotalSegmentator_v3 and the CTs sit one level deeper (Totalsegmentator_dataset_v300/s####/ct.nii.gz); the disk holds 1,939. Fix CT_ROOTS to the v300 folder.
- SchwingNet/CLAIM_CHECKLIST.md row 8 says the criterion behind the 142-subject list 'is not defined in any document read'; it is defined in the tools/ts_clean_subjects.py docstring (C2-L5 + sacrum each >= 200 voxels, < 20 voxels above 2,500 HU) and in docs/DEEPFLUORO_FORMATION.md line 135 (156 of 1,939 carry C2 + sacrum). The prose needs copying into DATASHEET section 3, not a new criterion.
- SchwingNet/NUMBERS.md line 111 'TotalSegmentator 1,228 -> 1,170 rendered -> 142 clean -> 17 validation' states a lineage that is false: the 142 were selected from the 1,939 v3 CTs directly, not from the 1,170 v2 renders (44 of the 142 are in ts_drr v2).
- SchwingNet/tables/tab_corpus.md gives TotalSegmentator v2 and v3 'Contributes: level bank' and counts 1,735 v3 renders in the 4,093-render total; no bank, split or arm reads ts_drr_v3 (PIS section 2: 'ts_drr_v3 is not among the keyer's roots at all'; sweep_v3/ts sources = ~/ts_drr). What TS contributes to the level bank is physics_drr (1,831 renders, 1,121 v2 subjects) and implant_ts (817 v2 subje
- docs/SWEEP_PROTOCOL.md 'On 522 routine clinical CTs ... which no model here was trained on' is true for the model the arms used (model_r18_ce_v3.pt, holdout bank allspine_v3_fov96_ctx) and false for the v7-v9 encoders, whose fold bank (MODEL_CARD section 3.2) contains physics_drr with 1,121 TS subjects; the sentence must name the model.
- Memory record spine-data-review-and-ts-bench: '570 have a sacrum, 219 a C2, 44 both' versus sweep.json spines 522 and 203 with only 1 and 2 recorded skips: 48 and 16 renders unaccounted for on disk.
- tools/ts_to_drr.py docstring cites v2 as 'Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.6802613' (the v1 record); v2.0.1 is 10.5281/zenodo.10047292 (WebFetch confirmed 1,228 CTs there).
- docs/DATASHEET.md section 2.7 and RSNAI_DATA_MANIFEST.json give 52.3 GB for the TS CTs; du -sh gives 86G (allocated blocks on Panasas vs summed apparent sizes). Not a data gap, but the two numbers should not be quoted as if they were one.
- ~/ts_drr/render_report.json no longer describes the render (it was overwritten by the --path-only pass and now lists 769 'not in corpus' v3 ids); the 1,228 -> 1,170 breakdown in docs (58 skipped) is right, but the per-id reasons exist only in ~/model_night/logs/tsdrr_40384840.out (first 40) and in the set difference computed here.

Documents that disagree with the disk:
- MODEL_CARD.md §3.2 / SchwingNet tab_corpus footnote h treat 'VINDRPS 2632' (and 'csxa 1758', 'buu_full 635', 'nhanes 303') as in-bank counts; AUDIT.md shows they are per-source CROP counts of the 31,446-crop capped mix bank (the twelve numbers sum to 31,446). The disk is right; the footnote's '2,632 films' reading is wrong.
- SchwingNet tab_corpus BUU-LSPINE row: 'level bank; validation | 3,603 lateral and 3,600 AP films' and the 12,709-film total include 3,600 AP films that are in no level bank (v9bank log: buu_full 3,603 cases only; AP lives only in films_ap_drr/buu_pair_fov48_ctx). Disk is right.
- docs/DATASHEET.md §2.6 and docs/XRAY_DATASET_USAGE.md: NHANES II 'on disk 17,073' with no note that the release lists 17,075; CDC serves L00452/L00456 as 0-byte files, so 17,073 is the complete non-empty release — the docs should say so rather than imply completeness by silence; slurm download_nhanes_full.sh header says '17,075 TIFF' and its loop can never finish.
- tools/xray_dataset_usage.py RAW list (and XRAY_DATASET_USAGE.md rows 'AASCE test split | AP + lateral | 4,000 | 0 | report spreadsheet only' and 'AASCE test split | 122 | the challenge withheld the landmarks') mislabels ~/spine-detector/data/raw/buu_2000, which is the BUU-LSPINE(2000) release (2,000 AP+LA pairs, pixels identical to buu_full, 1,337 csv relabelled). Disk is right.
- ~/vindr_pseudo/pseudolabel_report.json says 'images: 2077, kept: 2037' but the ann dir holds 10,274 VINDRPS files: vindr_label.sh ran train_images then test_images and the second pass overwrote the report. The ann dir is right; the report under-states by 8,237 (job 40416360 writes in_train/in_test membership).
- docs/PATIENT_IDENTITY_SPEC.md §2 vindr row keeps 'vindr_pseudo 10,274 [UNCERTAIN — a second directory ~/vindr_pseudo_drr was counted at 744; recount both]': recounted — both are right and are different objects (10,274 anns in vindr_pseudo from all 10,466 images; 744 anns in vindr_pseudo_drr from the 2,328 lateral-only vindr_det_drr route).
- Zenodo 10223910 page text says '600 of them annotated' and the VertXNet paper says 544; the shipped zip contains 543 pkl (275 C + 268 L, job 40416364 unzip -l). DATASHEET's 543 is right.
- slurm/overnight_v7.sh says 'VinDr contributes 5,369 lumbar films of which 1,127 have the sacrum boxed' while vindr_pseudo holds 10,274 anns (cervical films included) and FILTER_SUMMARY.json counts 1,127 sacrum-anchored + 1,771 rib-zone + 2,233 cervical kept + 5,143 dropped for VinDr; the 5,369 is the lumbar subset, not the bank's content.
- docs/RSNAI_DATA_MANIFEST.json (the hashed manifest the atlas card cites) lists no root for CSXA, BUU-LSPINE, NHANES II or VinDr at all; the plain-film corpora are counted only in prose.
- SchwingNet/results/a5.json v2_statement calls v8 'the clean v8 bank, which is the bank the manuscript must quote', while allspine_v9 (VerSe re-keyed) is the current bank and its film counts differ slightly (csxa val 749 vs 727, buu 533 vs 549, nhanes 79 vs 76) — outside this group's scope but the film numbers quoted come from v8.

Documents that disagree with the disk:
- DOI SWAP: docs/DATASHEET.md §3 ('the JHU Dataverse (DeepFluoro vendor DICOMs, doi:10.7281/T1/IFSXNV)'), docs/RSNAI_DATA_MANIFEST.json roots.deepfluoro_vendor.source and SchwingNet/atlas/dataset.json References all attribute the vendor DICOMs to IFSXNV. The Dataverse API (jhudv_40416362.out) shows IFSXNV is the DeepFluoro/IPCAI-2020 h5 release (ipcai_2020_full_res_data.zip 2.54 GB); the vendor DICO
- SchwingNet/tables/tab_corpus.md footnote (i) and tab_corpus.sources.md: 'anchor-crop (7,842) render banks … used by no trained component'. False: anchor_drr trained anchor_det_v1, which is the render-trained detector in the quoted 71.67 % vs 99.14 % comparison (SchwingNet/results/a3.json#comparisons/render_vs_film_detector: 'render-trained detector (anchor_det_v1, no real image)'; NUMBERS.md Plain
- docs/DATASHEET.md §2.6 and docs/XRAY_DATASET_USAGE.md give Spinal-AI2024 as 20,001 films; the release README and the disk hold 20,000 (5 x 4,000). The extra one is repo/figs/checklist.png swept up by rglob in tools/xray_dataset_usage.py count_images. MANIFEST_DATA_MODELS_RESULTS.md ('20,000 … not 20,001') is right.
- docs/XRAY_DATASET_USAGE.md row 'AASCE test split | AP + lateral | 4,000 | report spreadsheet only' points at ~/spine-detector/data/raw/buu_2000, which is BUU-LSPINE (AP/, LA/, 'Dataset Report.xlsx'), not AASCE (tools/xray_dataset_usage.py:56 maps key buu2000 to that name). Its other row 'AASCE test split | 122 unused films | the challenge withheld the landmarks' is 579 − 457 and conflates the 98 u
- Every local document reports AASCE from the grid copy (579 jpg = 481 + 98; MANIFEST_DATA_MODELS_RESULTS.md, DATASHEET §2.6 'on disk 579', PIS §2) as if it were the release; the release is 609 labelled + 98 test (AASCE2019 paper, and the mirror's 481 + 128 on the CephFS volume). No document says the level bank saw only 481 of 609 labelled films. MANIFEST also says '251 patients' where PIS §2 counts
- SpineSAC phantom count: DATASHEET §2.1 'raw frames of one sawbone phantom'; HANDOFF_2026-09-21_PM.md:442 '58 AP/LAT REAL C-arm pairs … from FIVE devices'; the README says 59 scenarios over three sawbone phantoms (one closed, two open) and the on-disk inventory.json holds 3 CT series and 59 scenarios across the six device headers the DATASHEET itself lists. README + inventory are right; the datashe
- Memory note grid-quota-and-deleted-banks says 'Split lists recovered to ~/bank_splits.json' and the banks are 'rebuildable by slurm/build_allspine_v*.sh'. bank_splits.json (2026-09-20 19:37) holds allspine_fov96_ctx, v6, v7, v7nofluoro, films_all2, films_ann only — none of v2–v5c; and build_allspine_v3.sh:18 / build_allspine_v4.sh:25 execute $H/tmp/build_allspine_v3.py, which the same sweep of ~/t
- slurm/disk_cleanup.sh header (2026-09-17) lists 'allspine_v2/v3/v4/v5 the crop banks every current model trains and validates on' under NOT DELETED; three days later they were deleted by an action with no log in ~/model_night/logs (no cleanup file dated Sep 20). The deletion is recorded only in memory and in DATA_ACCOUNTING.md §8.
- Mendeley 5jdfdgp762: the release page says 716 images (494/206/16) while its only file, Dataset.zip (43,892,677 bytes, complete on disk), holds 714 (494/204/16). MANIFEST's '714 — not 716' and DATASHEET's 714 are right; the release's own description is off by two. ## Independent re-counts (the verifications that ran)

### COBRA2026 (Thummerer et al. 2026, arXiv:2607.20037; Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.21322350; CC BY-NC 4.0) as CONSUMED: the cobra_elekta bank and its four derived banks, the crossdev bins, the whole-spine bank

**Verdict.** The auditor's release and disk numbers hold to the frame (867/692/52/123; 375,618 public projections and every per-centre sum; 150 attempted, 145 kept, 3,480 = 24 x 145; the derived banks; the box drops; the landmark gates), and its central answer is right: 24 frames per case and 150 of 467 cases are budget cuts with no written criterion, exactly as DATASHEET §3 and RESUME D4 already say. Four of its statements are wrong: the Elekta card was optimised on 40 of 335 frames from 12 cases (pairs_dir or_ready/devices/elekta_xvi/pairs), not fitted on 3 cases; a Varian card (526 frames, 12 centre-G cases, held-out R2 0.446, misnamed 'halcyon') exists untracked on the grid; the '208 / 202 / 238' are 30-degree bands over the 648 test frames, not the crossdev bins (whose test parts are 102 / 202 / 132); and B000 is a release-side defect (ct_def released 0.3-0.5 m outside the FOV), which the docs describe three different ways. Only two COBRA gaps rest on a written scientific criterion (the ap45 photon-starved-lateral rule and the pelvis-landmark gates); the 0.10 axis-map margin is written but unjustified and excludes at least one case (A239, NCC 0.762) that is almost certainly fine; Varian no

- Elekta device card fitted on 40 frames ('fit_frames': 40) from pairs of 3 cases (A002, A004, A005); cobra2026/pairs has 6 cases only (A002 23, A004 24, A005 29, F011 26, F041 26, F051 26); frames.csv  → verifier got cobra2026/pairs counts are right (EVERY=16 pilot run, 6 cases), but that is NOT  (`sed -n 3405,3415p and 3476,3492p docs/devices/elekta_xvi.json; ssh grid 'ls .../or_ready/devices/elekta_xvi/pairs | sed -E s/^pair_\([A-G][0-9]{3}\)_.*/\\1/ | sort | uniq -c; head -c 700 .../pairs/pai`)
- No varian_halcyon.json exists in docs/devices/; Varian has no export bank and contributes nothing → verifier got True of the LOCAL checkout only. On the grid ~/spinesurg-ct-nnunet/docs/devices/ (`ssh grid 'ls ~/spinesurg-ct-nnunet/docs/devices/; grep -o n_frames_total/fit_frames/date/model/held_out_r2_median ~/spinesurg-ct-nnunet/docs/devices/varian_halcyon.json; ls .../or_ready/devices/varian`)
- Cross-device level test bins cobra_ap / cobra_obl / cobra_lat = cobra_elekta TEST frames with |lat| <= 15 / 30-60 / >= 75 = 208 / 202 / 238 frames (NUMBERS.md line 81) → verifier got Wrong mapping. The crossdev bins (or_ready/crossdev/bins, tools/or_crossdev_bins (`ssh grid 'grep -l "\"split\": \"test\"" cobra_elekta/meta/*.json | xargs grep -h -o "\"lat_from_ap_deg\": [-0-9.]*" | grep -o "[-0-9.]*$" | sed s/^-// > /dev/shm/lat.txt; grep -c -E "^([0-9]|1[0-4])(\`)

Reclassified gaps:
- 24 frames per case: 3,480 frames exported of 77,622 fetched (4.5 %) and of 232,103 public Elekta frames (1.5 %); N_FRAMES = 24, frames = round(arange(24) * nproj / 24): auditor **not stated in the excerpt I was given (the gaps section was cut off); the auditor asked 'why 24' without answering** → verifier **budget** — The only written text is the docstring '24 frames evenly over the orbit (laterals included)' and summary.json frames_per_case 24; no document gives a scientific reason for 24 (15 degrees on a full orbit, ~8 on a half orbit). An angular subsample COULD be justified in writing (frames ~1 degree apart at 5.5 fps are near-duplicates; DATASHEET already records 74,364 cross-case near-duplicate pairs), b
- 150 of 467 public Elekta cases attempted (cases150.txt, N = 40/40/40/30 stride over the full sorted lists): auditor **budget (the auditor quoted summary.json 'selection' without a class)** → verifier **budget** — docs/DATASHEET.md §3 states it in so many words: 'The cut was download volume (42 GB for the 152 Elekta cases) and no criterion'. The stride was over the full lists, so this cut is independent of the cases[:40] display bug that capped the later full fetch. Repair in progress: job 40416315 fetches all 692; the export must then be re-run on the full list (RESUME A10/D4).

**Verdict.** The union of VerSe'19+'20 is complete at the series level: 374/374 series with CT+mask, 355 subjects, nothing dropped for cervical-only fields or failed conversion, 0 download failures, and every whole-spine bank that matters (verse_drr, soft, film, obl, obl2, anchor_drr, vf_813) covers all 374. The 85-vs-50 L6 difference is the release's written Castellvi III/IV rule plus one FOV case -- not our truncation -- but it means the label-25-driven rare-render arm never sees the 35 partially fused lumbosacral spines the safety net exists for. - Centroid *_ctd.json fetched but not carried into data/unified: auditor **not classified** → verifier **criterion (written)** — unify.py docstring: 'the X/Y/Z values use INCONSISTENT coordinate conventions across the corpus ... DO NOT materialize them in data/unified/ -- downstream stages compute per-vertebra centroids fresh from the segmentation mask'. The files remain in data/raw. Not a truncation.

Gaps the auditor missed:
- 10 VerSe'20 series (verse020, 112, 139, 141, 151, 202, 235, 251, 257, 290) are held at their VerSe'19 annotation although VerSe'20 re-released them with corrected labels; download.py's BIDS fallback only triggers for series absent from BOTH MICCAI nodes (bug, repairable from osf.io/4skx2 rawdata+derivatives or the S3 zips); design.md says the S3 zips are the source and the code used OSF MICCAI ins
- 37 series of the 18 multi-series subjects (verse400-417) are never rendered for the LSTV flag: TRANSITIONAL_LABELS.csv keys them by subject id and lstv_flag_render_verse.py opens scan-<case_id> (FileNotFoundError on every shard log); lstv_flag_labels_v2.csv carries the same 18 subject-id rows as if they were volumes (293 series + 18 phantoms = 311 'verse' rows); rendered-but-unlabelled verse065 an
- 30 GL series are outside every VerSe transitional label set because Suppl Table 1 covers only the 325 TUM subjects; a legitimate limitation, but written nowhere in the repo (the tool docstring only says the label 'comes from the radiologist reading in Loffler's Supplemental Table 1').
- The VerSe'20 BIDS node ships fracture_data/Genant_Fractures_thoracolumbar_reference.csv (11,823 B, osf.io/4skx2) which was never fetched; VerSeFusion/docs/design.md declares fracture grading out of scope ('a separate downstream pipeline') -- a scope statement, not a scientific criterion; irrelevant to level naming.
- implant_verse holds 373 of 374 (verse116 absent, mask L5 only) and docs/MANIFEST line 187 quotes 373 without naming or explaining the drop; no 'skip verse116' line in any impverse log, so the loss is silent (class unknown).
- physics_rare's 9 unrendered rare scans include 2 of the only 6 T13 volumes in existence here (verse602, verse631); the rare-class arm therefore trains on 4 T13 CTs, not 6.
- Docs vs disk: MANIFEST line 184 '463 raw' (disk 469 series-copies, 1,897 files); merge_versefusion_corpus.py '373 VerSeFusion cases' (its own log and Dataset815 say 374); convert_versefusion.py '44 cases with an L6 and 18 with a T13' (raw masks 50 / 6); tab_corpus/atlas '387 renders' = 374 distinct + 13 byte-identical duplicates that the v9 bank ingests twice; auditor's Castellvi '0/1 146' vs 181 
- No truncated downloads: 0 failed, 26 cached at expected size, smallest CT 1.72 MB; the two tiny masks (verse201_seg 44.9 KB, verse278_seg 26.8 KB) are byte-identical across releases, so they are small scans, not broken files.

## What this audit still owes

- Verification did not run for the groups not listed above (the model limit stopped them): their gap
  classes rest on one agent's reading.
- No repair job was launched by the audit. Every repair above is a plan, not a running job.
- FluoroCap is dropped from training by PI decision, so its gaps need no repair — only the datasheet line.


## Repairs

### 2026-09-23 — the two film corpora: NHANES II (D2) and VinDr-SpineXR (D3)

Jobs **40416535** (`slurm/film_corpus_inventory.sh`) and **40416559** (`slurm/nhanes_box_convention.sh`),
both resumable, both writing to `/rs/rs_grp_oschome/go2432/data/inventory/films/`. Recorded in
`SchwingNet/results/JOBS_data_repairs.txt`.

**NHANES II — the two absent entries are the CDC's, not ours (row: "2 of 17,075 release entries absent").**
`L00452.tiff` and `L00456.tiff` are served by `ftp.cdc.gov` as HTTP 200 with `Content-Length: 0`, last
modified 2025-08-14, and are listed at size 0 in the index. Confirmed twice from a compute node by HEAD and
GET — job 40416367 on the first pass, job 40416535 independently — and `L00451.tiff` fetched beside them
returns 6,561,246 bytes while `L00453.tiff` returns 404, so the empty pair is not a transport artefact.
Nothing is downloadable; 17,073 is the whole fetchable release. `L00452` is one of the 543 annotated films
and its pixels survive inside the Zenodo pickle. Repair: `nhanes_full/.download_complete` is now written
with the reason, so `slurm/download_nhanes_full.sh` — whose non-empty test could never pass over two empty
files, and which resubmitted itself until it was cancelled — is never relaunched.

**NHANES II — the usable inventory the corpus builder was missing (row: "16,530 unannotated films in no
trained bank"). LANDED (job 40416535, 03:00).** 17,075 manifest rows; 17,073 on disk (9,667 cervical + 7,406
lumbar) over 9,698 sample persons; 543 annotated films (275 + 268) over 364 persons. **The unannotated count
is 16,531, not 16,530**: one annotated film, L00452, has no TIFF on disk because the CDC serves it empty, so
542 films on disk carry landmarks. The level bank still holds 543 NHANES films — that one reaches it from the
pickle, not from the TIFF — so nothing trained changes; only the count of films available to the detector. Every 16,530 in this project subtracted 543 from 17,073 and counted that
film twice; `docs/DATASHEET.md` §2.6 and `docs/MANIFEST_DATA_MODELS_RESULTS.md` are corrected. `tools/film_corpus_inventory.py` writes `nhanes_film_manifest.csv`, one row per release
entry: film id, view, person key, bytes on disk, whether the Zenodo landmarks cover it, the pseudo-label
state (ann file, how many levels, how many at the exact tier), which detector bank and which level bank
holds it, and a `usable_for` role. The level-bank exclusion of the unannotated films stays as it is — it is
written and was measured (raw decoder pseudolabels cost twenty points of anomalous accuracy). What had no
criterion was DETECTION, where class-agnostic boxes do not depend on a level name, and the manifest is what
the detector self-training corpus and the corpus-manifold plot (D2) now consume instead of re-deriving it.
Verify: `FILM_CORPUS_INVENTORY.md` in that directory, and the manifest row count equals the release index.
What the finished manifest says of the 17,073 films on disk (job 40416623, the rerun with the pseudo-label
parser fixed):

| role | films |
|---|---|
| in the level bank and the detector (annotated) | 542 |
| detection self-training, pseudo-label carries a named level | 15,253 |
| detection self-training, pseudo-label carries boxes but no name | 476 |
| detection self-training, never pseudo-labelled | 802 |

16,234 pseudo-label anns exist over the corpus, 15,746 of them with at least one named level — which is the
count `nhanesps_fov96_ctx` was built from, reached here by a different route. **The audit's "839 never
pseudo-labelled" is 802 when counted per film**; 839 was a difference of two totals, and the gate that
rejected them (fewer than two vertebrae detected) is unchanged.

**NHANES II — what the human truth actually boxes. MEASURED, and it is the body.** The standing box
convention (vertebral body) rests on the claim that human truth boxes bodies. NHANES is the one film corpus
in the detector bank whose boxes are the extent of a human mask (`tools/nhanes_to_drr_format.py`), so job
40416559 measured it instead of assuming it, over all 543 films and 3,224 masks:

- Median box width over height is **1.01**. Per level: cervical 0.75–0.93, lumbar 1.12–1.16. A vertebral
  body on a lateral is about as deep as it is tall, which is what those numbers are; a whole vertebra on the
  same film carries the pedicle, facets and spinous process behind the body and would read near 2.5. The
  two conventions are a factor of two apart on this number and the distribution sits squarely on one of them.
- The renders settle it (the project's own rule): `box_convention/*_masks.png` shows a four-corner
  quadrilateral on each vertebral body — T12 to S1 on the lumbar film, C2 to C6 on the cervical, C2's
  polygon taking in the odontoid — with nothing posterior to the body inside any mask.
- Mask keys across the 543 pickles: a combined `vertebrae` mask in every film (excluded by the converter),
  `C2`–`C7`, `T1`, `T11`, `T12`, `L1`–`L5`, `S1`, and a `C2_lower` in 272 films. The overall fill median of
  0.54 is a rotated quadrilateral inside an axis-aligned box plus the `C2_lower` slivers, not evidence about
  the convention; the per-level width-over-height is the number to read.

So the premise behind the standing decision holds for this corpus: the human boxes are body boxes, and
rendering bodies (SPINEPS) is what matches them. It is not established here for BUU-LSPINE (end-plate lines)
or for VinDr, whose vertebra boxes come from our own detector and carry whatever convention it was trained
on, not from a human.

**NHANES and VinDr — the usage table regenerated (jobs 40416579, 40416596, 40416597).**
`docs/XRAY_DATASET_USAGE.md` printed "VinDr-SpineXR | on disk 0 | in a bank 744 | allspine_v3_fov96_ctx":
the 0 was an extension its counter did not know (`.dicom`), and `allspine_v3`/`v4` were deleted in the
2026-09-20 sweep. Two fixes to `tools/xray_dataset_usage.py`: `.dicom` added, and a new column for the bank
the reported model was actually trained on, because the old "which bank" column showed whichever bank held
the most films — for NHANES that is `nhanesps_fov96_ctx` with 15,746 pseudo-label films, and reading it as
the shipped bank is exactly how one dataset came to have three numbers. It now reads: NHANES 17,073 on disk,
543 in the shipped bank, 15,746 in some bank; VinDr 10,466 on disk, **0** in the shipped bank, 7,625 in some
bank. That is an independent route to the same answer as the bank case arrays.

**VinDr-SpineXR — three numbers, three objects (rows: "in-bank count printed three ways" and "0 of 10,466
in the current level banks").** Established from each object itself (job 40416442 over the bank case arrays,
job 40416535 per released image):

| object | count |
|---|---|
| release on disk | 10,466 images / 5,000 studies; every SHA-256 verified, 0 mismatches (job 40416364) |
| `~/vindr_pseudo/ann` | 10,274 ann files (192 images never pseudo-labelled) |
| of those, carrying a named level | 7,625 — the other 2,649 have an empty levels dict and make no crop |
